Reference items in this project, not the total surviving archive. One reference can belong to several record types or countries, and several references can describe the same record. Countries come from source descriptions and recorded places; undated and ambiguous items stay in the list. Select a row for its details.   • Identity, trade and civic status Niniane Glasgw was a fyscher and burges of Glasgw — a fisher and burgess of Glasgow. The occupation was formerly misread as flescher, meaning butcher. That reading should be withdrawn. The manuscript word is fyscher. An independent burgess list also places Niniane Glasgow under the heading Marinellis and Fischeris, meaning mariners and fishers. George Crawfurd, A Sketch of the Rise
